"""Tests for MainWindow (piece 7.6b).

Exercise the Home <-> Workspace navigation cycle (ADR-0006):

- HomePage.project_open_requested creates a Workspace, calls
  ProjectManager.open_project, injects the "Home" button into the
  TopOverlay, and switches the QStackedWidget.
- Clicking the "Home" button calls ProjectManager.close_project,
  destroys the Workspace, and returns to the HomePage.
- Placeholder actions show QMessageBox.information.
- A repository failure (e.g. ProjectNotFound) shows QMessageBox.critical
  and leaves the HomePage as the current widget.

Uses InMemoryProjectRepository to avoid filesystem I/O, a real EventBus,
and AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy. No real Workspace spec is loaded
beyond the default placeholder, so the tests do not depend on layouts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox, QPushButton, QStackedWidget
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus
from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_catalog import ProjectCatalog
from cranioz.application.project.project_manager import ProjectManager
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_registry import EditorRegistry
from cranioz.ui.main_window import MainWindow
from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeName, ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.toggle_tool import ToggleTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import ToolRegistry

_ACTOR = "test"


# ----- Fixtures -----------------------------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def manager_and_catalog() -> tuple[ProjectManager, ProjectCatalog]:
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repo)
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=EventBus(),
        access_policy=AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy(),
    )
    return manager, catalog


@pytest.fixture
def window(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    manager_and_catalog: tuple[ProjectManager, ProjectCatalog],
) -> MainWindow:
    manager, catalog = manager_and_catalog

    editor_registry = EditorRegistry()
    tool_registry = ToolRegistry()
    tool_registry.register("tool.log", LogTool)
    tool_registry.register("tool.toggle", ToggleTool)
    theme_service = ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.DARK)

    w = MainWindow(
        catalog=catalog,
        manager=manager,
        actor=_ACTOR,
        editor_registry=editor_registry,
        tool_registry=tool_registry,
        theme_service=theme_service,
    )
    qtbot.addWidget(w)
    return w


@pytest.fixture
def seeded_manager(
    manager_and_catalog: tuple[ProjectManager, ProjectCatalog],
) -> ProjectManager:
    manager, _ = manager_and_catalog
    manager.create_project(
        _ACTOR,
        project_id=ProjectId("p-001"),
        project_name="Case 001",
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
    )
    return manager


def _stack(window: MainWindow) -> QStackedWidget:
    stack = window.findChild(QStackedWidget)
    assert stack is not None, "MainWindow must host a QStackedWidget"
    return stack


# ----- Initial state ------------------------------------------------


def test_home_page_is_initial_widget(window: MainWindow) -> None:
    assert window.current_workspace() is None
    assert window.current_project_id() is None
    assert _stack(window).currentWidget() is window.home()


def test_window_has_expected_title(window: MainWindow) -> None:
    assert window.windowTitle() == "CranioZ"


# ----- Open project -------------------------------------------------


def test_open_project_creates_workspace(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )

    workspace = window.current_workspace()
    assert workspace is not None
    assert window.current_project_id() == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert workspace.project_context.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_open_project_switches_stack(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )

    assert _stack(window).currentWidget() is window.current_workspace()


def test_open_project_injects_home_button(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )

    workspace = window.current_workspace()
    assert workspace is not None
    home_buttons = [
        btn
        for btn in workspace.top_overlay().left_segment().findChildren(QPushButton)
        if btn.text() == "Home"
    ]
    assert len(home_buttons) == 1
    assert home_buttons[0].objectName() == "workspace.home_button"


def test_open_project_loads_default_spec(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    """The Workspace is loaded with the default (placeholder) spec."""
    window.home().refresh()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )

    workspace = window.current_workspace()
    assert workspace is not None
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is True
    assert [a.area_id for a in workspace.areas()] == ["area.central"]


# ----- Close project (Home button) ----------------------------------


def test_home_button_closes_workspace(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()
    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )
    assert window.current_workspace() is not None

    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()

    assert window.current_workspace() is None
    assert window.current_project_id() is None


def test_home_button_returns_to_home_page(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()
    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )

    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()

    assert _stack(window).currentWidget() is window.home()


def test_home_button_does_not_close_another_project(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    """Clicking Home closes only the currently opened project."""
    seeded_manager.create_project(
        _ACTOR,
        project_id=ProjectId("p-002"),
        project_name="Case 002",
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-002"),
        patient_display_name="Maria Souza",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
    )
    window.home().refresh()

    # Open p-001, close it, then open p-002. Only p-002 is closed.
    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )
    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-002"), "orthognathic"
    )
    assert window.current_project_id() == ProjectId("p-002")

    # Open again: p-002 should be the one closed.
    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()
    assert window.current_project_id() is None


# ----- Error handling -----------------------------------------------


def test_open_missing_project_shows_error(
    window: MainWindow,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    def fake_critical(parent, title, text):  # noqa: ANN001
        calls.append((title, text))

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        QMessageBox, "critical", staticmethod(fake_critical)
    )

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-999"), "orthognathic"
    )

    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert calls[0][0] == "Cannot open project"
    assert window.current_workspace() is None
    assert _stack(window).currentWidget() is window.home()


# ----- Placeholder actions ------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("signal_name", "expected_title"),
    [
        ("new_project_requested", "New project"),
        ("manage_projects_requested", "Manage projects"),
        ("manage_flows_requested", "Manage flows"),
        ("settings_requested", "Settings"),
        ("help_requested", "Help"),
        ("community_requested", "Community"),
        ("library_requested", "Object library"),
        ("news_requested", "News"),
        ("feedback_requested", "Suggest improvements"),
        ("contribute_requested", "Contribute"),
    ],
)
def test_placeholder_shows_information(
    window: MainWindow,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    signal_name: str,
    expected_title: str,
) -> None:
    calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    def fake_information(parent, title, text):  # noqa: ANN001
        calls.append((title, text))

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        QMessageBox, "information", staticmethod(fake_information)
    )

    signal = getattr(window.home(), signal_name)
    signal.emit()

    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert calls[0][0] == expected_title


# ----- Lifecycle ----------------------------------------------------


def test_destroy_workspace_after_close(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """After Home is clicked, the old Workspace is no longer reachable."""
    window.home().refresh()
    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )
    workspace = window.current_workspace()
    assert workspace is not None

    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()

    # The workspace reference is cleared. The object may not be deleted
    # immediately (deleteLater), but the MainWindow no longer exposes it.
    assert window.current_workspace() is None


def test_open_after_close_creates_a_new_workspace(
    window: MainWindow,
    seeded_manager: ProjectManager,
) -> None:
    window.home().refresh()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )
    first = window.current_workspace()
    assert first is not None

    home_button = window.findChild(QPushButton, "workspace.home_button")
    assert home_button is not None
    home_button.click()

    window.home().project_open_requested.emit(
        ProjectId("p-001"), "orthognathic"
    )
    second = window.current_workspace()
    assert second is not None
    assert second is not first



======================================================================
FILE: tests/integration/ui/workspace/areas/test_area_widget.py
======================================================================

"""Integration tests for AreaWidget.

The AreaWidget is the first widget in CranioZ that materializes the
contents of a single Area: its Editors' Toolbars, their content
widgets, and tabs when there are multiple Editors.

These tests use qtbot: an AreaWidget is a real QWidget, and the
Editors it hosts (DummyEditor) return a QLabel as content. A
QApplication is therefore required.

What these tests cover:

- Empty Area: placeholder widget is shown.
- Single-Editor Area: no tabs, content is rendered directly.
- Single-Editor Area: Toolbars are materialized.
- Multi-Editor Area: a QTabWidget is created with the expected tabs.
- Tab position is South (bottom of the Area).
- Tabs are closable.
- The active tab matches the Area's active_editor_id.
- Switching tabs emits active_editor_changed.
- Closing a tab emits editor_close_requested.
- The widget exposes its area_id.
- refresh() rebuilds the widget from the Area model.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel, QTabWidget

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeName, ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.base import Toolbar
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.toggle_tool import ToggleTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import ToolRegistry
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import Area
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area_widget import AreaWidget
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement

# ----- Local helpers ------------------------------------------------


def _area(
    area_id: str = "area.central",
    title: str = "Central",
    placement: Placement = Placement.CENTRAL,
) -> Area:
    return Area(area_id=area_id, title=title, placement=placement)


def _add_editor(
    area: Area,
    editor_id: str,
    title: str | None = None,
    toolbar_orientation=None,
) -> DummyEditor:
    """Create a DummyEditor, add it to the Area, and return it.

    The DummyEditor declares one ToolbarSpec with tool.log and
    tool.toggle. This is enough to exercise Toolbar materialization
    inside the AreaWidget.
    """
    kwargs = {"editor_id": editor_id, "title": title or editor_id}
    if toolbar_orientation is not None:
        kwargs["toolbar_orientation"] = toolbar_orientation
    editor = DummyEditor(**kwargs)
    area.add_editor(editor)
    return editor


# ----- Fixtures local to this module --------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
    """A ToolRegistry with LogTool and ToggleTool registered."""
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", LogTool)
    registry.register("tool.toggle", ToggleTool)
    return registry


@pytest.fixture
def theme_service() -> ThemeService:
    """Default ThemeService (dark theme)."""
    return ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.DARK)


# ----- Empty Area ---------------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_creates_placeholder_when_empty(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    labels = widget.findChildren(QLabel)
    assert len(labels) == 1
    assert "empty" in labels[0].text().lower()


def test_area_widget_placeholder_includes_area_title(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area(title="Central")
    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    labels = widget.findChildren(QLabel)
    assert len(labels) == 1
    assert "Central" in labels[0].text()


def test_area_widget_empty_has_no_tabs(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    assert widget.findChild(QTabWidget) is None


# ----- Single-Editor Area -------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_no_tabs_with_one_editor(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.patient")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    assert widget.findChild(QTabWidget) is None


def test_area_widget_renders_content_widget_of_single_editor(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    editor = _add_editor(area, "editor.patient")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    content = editor.content_widget()
    # The content widget is a QLabel whose text is the editor_id.
    # It must be a descendant of the AreaWidget.
    assert content is not None
    assert widget.isAncestorOf(content)


def test_area_widget_renders_toolbars_of_single_editor(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.patient")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    toolbars = widget.findChildren(Toolbar)
    assert len(toolbars) == 1
    # DummyEditor declares tool.log and tool.toggle.
    assert set(toolbars[0].tool_ids()) == {"tool.log", "tool.toggle"}


# ----- Multi-Editor Area --------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_tabs_with_multiple_editors(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None
    assert tabs.count() == 2


def test_area_widget_tab_labels_match_editor_titles(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None
    assert tabs.tabText(0) == "Alpha"
    assert tabs.tabText(1) == "Beta"


def test_area_widget_tab_position_is_south(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None
    assert tabs.tabPosition() == QTabWidget.TabPosition.South


def test_area_widget_tabs_are_closable(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None
    assert tabs.tabsClosable() is True


def test_area_widget_active_tab_matches_active_editor(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")
    area.set_active_editor("editor.b")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None
    assert tabs.currentIndex() == 1  # editor.b is at index 1


# ----- Signals ------------------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_emits_active_editor_changed_on_tab_switch(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None

    with qtbot.waitSignal(widget.active_editor_changed, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        tabs.setCurrentIndex(1)

    assert blocker.args == ["area.central", "editor.b"]


def test_area_widget_emits_close_requested_on_tab_close(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.a", title="Alpha")
    _add_editor(area, "editor.b", title="Beta")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    tabs = widget.findChild(QTabWidget)
    assert tabs is not None

    with qtbot.waitSignal(widget.editor_close_requested, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        # Emit the signal as if the user clicked the 'X' on the first tab.
        tabs.tabCloseRequested.emit(0)

    assert blocker.args == ["area.central", "editor.a"]


# ----- Metadata -----------------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_exposes_area_id(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    area = _area(area_id="area.left", title="Left", placement=Placement.LEFT)
    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    assert widget.area_id == "area.left"


# ----- Refresh ------------------------------------------------------


def test_area_widget_refresh_rebuilds_from_area(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    """refresh() reflects changes to the Area model.

    We start with an empty Area, verify the placeholder is shown,
    then add an Editor and call refresh(). The widget must now show
    the Editor's content and no placeholder.
    """
    area = _area()
    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    labels_before = widget.findChildren(QLabel)
    assert len(labels_before) == 1
    assert "empty" in labels_before[0].text().lower()

    editor = _add_editor(area, "editor.patient")
    widget.refresh()

    # After refresh: no placeholder, content present, one toolbar.
    labels_after = widget.findChildren(QLabel)
    # The content widget of DummyEditor is itself a QLabel. We expect
    # exactly one QLabel now (the content), not the placeholder.
    assert len(labels_after) == 1
    assert "empty" not in labels_after[0].text().lower()
    assert widget.isAncestorOf(editor.content_widget())

    toolbars = widget.findChildren(Toolbar)
    assert len(toolbars) == 1


def test_area_widget_refresh_idempotent(
    qtbot, tool_registry: ToolRegistry, theme_service: ThemeService
) -> None:
    """Calling refresh() twice with no model change produces the same structure."""
    area = _area()
    _add_editor(area, "editor.patient")

    widget = AreaWidget(area, tool_registry, theme_service)
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)

    toolbars_before = widget.findChildren(Toolbar)
    widget.refresh()
    toolbars_after = widget.findChildren(Toolbar)

    assert len(toolbars_before) == len(toolbars_after) == 1



======================================================================
FILE: tests/integration/ui/workspace/layout/test_layout_builder.py
======================================================================

"""Integration tests for LayoutBuilder.

These tests exercise the full path:

    ModuleUISpec
        → LayoutBuilder.build()
        → BuildResult
              ├── Area instances (with Editors)
              ├── LayoutTree
              ├── AreaManager (populated)
              └── editors_by_area

The LayoutBuilder is a pure composition step and does NOT touch Qt.
Therefore, unlike the widget tests, these tests do NOT require a
QApplication. They run without ``qtbot`` and without ``qapp``.

The DummyEditor used in these tests does use QWidget internally
(its content_widget() returns a QLabel), but the LayoutBuilder never
calls content_widget(). It only calls:

- EditorRegistry.create()          → constructs the instance
- Editor.initialize(context)       → stores the context
- Editor.editor_id / .title        → reads metadata

Since DummyEditor.initialize() does not instantiate any QWidget, the
builder runs without a QApplication. This is the property that makes
the LayoutBuilder testable in milliseconds and free of Qt platform
constraints.

What these tests cover:

- Building a spec with a single CENTRAL Area.
- Building a spec with LEFT + CENTRAL + RIGHT Areas.
- Building a spec with all four Areas (LEFT, CENTRAL, RIGHT, BOTTOM).
- Building a spec where a single Area hosts a nested 2x2 SPLIT of
  Editors (the prototype's central grid).
- The AreaManager is populated with every Area created.
- editors_by_area maps each area_id to the Editors it hosts, in
  depth-first order.
- BuildResult.area() and BuildResult.editors() accessors.
- Unknown editor_id → KeyError with a clear message.
- context_extras are copied per Editor (hybrid EditorContext model,
  ADR-0002 §2.1).
- shared_context is accepted without error but is not yet propagated
  (ADR-0002 §2.3).
- Invalid argument types raise TypeError.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_registry import EditorRegistry
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area import AreaError
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_builder import (
    BuildResult,
    LayoutBuilder,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_tree import Leaf, Split
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import (
    EditorLayoutSpec,
    SplitDirection,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.module_ui_spec import (
    ModuleUISpec,
    ModuleUISpecError,
)

# ----- Local helpers ------------------------------------------------


def _leaf(editor_id: str) -> EditorLayoutSpec:
    """Shorthand for a LEAF EditorLayoutSpec."""
    return EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id)


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement,
    editor_layout: EditorLayoutSpec,
    title: str | None = None,
) -> AreaSpec:
    """Build a minimal AreaSpec for tests."""
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=title if title is not None else area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=editor_layout,
    )


def _spec(*areas: AreaSpec) -> ModuleUISpec:
    """Build a ModuleUISpec from the given AreaSpecs."""
    return ModuleUISpec(areas=tuple(areas))


# ----- Fixtures local to this module --------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def registry() -> EditorRegistry:
    """An EditorRegistry pre-populated with the editor_ids used below.

    A single DummyEditor class is registered under several editor_ids
    so that the builder can create independent instances for each
    editor_id appearing in a spec. This mirrors the future reality
    where several distinct Editor classes will be registered under
    distinct ids.
    """
    reg = EditorRegistry()
    for editor_id in (
        "editor.patient",
        "editor.viewport_3d",
        "editor.mpr_axial",
        "editor.mpr_sagittal",
        "editor.mpr_coronal",
        "editor.object_list",
        "editor.properties",
        "editor.material",
        "editor.console",
        "editor.history",
        "editor.ai_assistant",
    ):
        reg.register(editor_id, DummyEditor)
    return reg


@pytest.fixture
def builder(registry: EditorRegistry) -> LayoutBuilder:
    """A LayoutBuilder backed by the populated registry."""
    return LayoutBuilder(registry)


# ----- Building a single CENTRAL Area -------------------------------


def test_build_single_central_creates_leaf_tree(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert isinstance(result, BuildResult)
    assert len(result.areas) == 1
    assert result.areas[0].area_id == "area.central"
    assert isinstance(result.tree.root, Leaf)
    assert result.tree.root.area.area_id == "area.central"


def test_build_single_central_registers_area(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert result.area_manager.contains("area.central")
    assert result.area_manager.area_count() == 1
    assert result.area_manager.has_central()


def test_build_single_central_creates_editor(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    editors = result.editors("area.central")
    assert len(editors) == 1
    assert editors[0].editor_id == "editor.patient"
    assert isinstance(editors[0], DummyEditor)


# ----- Building LEFT + CENTRAL + RIGHT ------------------------------


def test_build_left_central_right_tree_shape(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert isinstance(result.tree.root, Split)
    assert result.tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert len(result.tree.root.children) == 3
    assert [a.area_id for a in result.tree.areas()] == [
        "area.left",
        "area.central",
        "area.right",
    ]


def test_build_left_central_right_registers_all_areas(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert result.area_manager.area_count() == 3
    assert result.area_manager.contains("area.left")
    assert result.area_manager.contains("area.central")
    assert result.area_manager.contains("area.right")


def test_build_left_central_right_populates_editors(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert [e.editor_id for e in result.editors("area.left")] == [
        "editor.object_list",
    ]
    assert [e.editor_id for e in result.editors("area.central")] == [
        "editor.viewport_3d",
    ]
    assert [e.editor_id for e in result.editors("area.right")] == [
        "editor.properties",
    ]


# ----- Building all four Areas --------------------------------------


def test_build_four_areas_tree_shape(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
        _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, _leaf("editor.console")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    # Root is VERTICAL because there is a bottom row.
    assert isinstance(result.tree.root, Split)
    assert result.tree.root.direction is SplitDirection.VERTICAL
    top, bottom = result.tree.root.children
    assert isinstance(top, Split)
    assert top.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert isinstance(bottom, Leaf)
    assert bottom.area.area_id == "area.bottom"


def test_build_four_areas_registers_all(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
        _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, _leaf("editor.console")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert result.area_manager.area_count() == 4
    for area_id in ("area.left", "area.central", "area.right", "area.bottom"):
        assert result.area_manager.contains(area_id)


# ----- A single Area hosting a nested 2x2 SPLIT ---------------------


def test_build_central_with_2x2_split(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    """Replicates the prototype's central grid: 2x2 of MPR + 3D."""
    central_layout = EditorLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.VERTICAL,
                (
                    _leaf("editor.mpr_axial"),
                    _leaf("editor.mpr_sagittal"),
                ),
            ),
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.VERTICAL,
                (
                    _leaf("editor.mpr_coronal"),
                    _leaf("editor.viewport_3d"),
                ),
            ),
        ),
    )
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, central_layout),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    # The tree has a single Area (CENTRAL), so the root is a Leaf.
    assert isinstance(result.tree.root, Leaf)
    assert result.tree.area_count() == 1

    # The single Area hosts four Editors, in depth-first order.
    editors = result.editors("area.central")
    assert [e.editor_id for e in editors] == [
        "editor.mpr_axial",
        "editor.mpr_sagittal",
        "editor.mpr_coronal",
        "editor.viewport_3d",
    ]


def test_build_central_split_editor_order_is_depth_first(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """Order of Editors is the depth-first order of the EditorLayoutSpec."""
    central_layout = EditorLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (
            _leaf("editor.mpr_axial"),
            _leaf("editor.mpr_sagittal"),
            _leaf("editor.mpr_coronal"),
        ),
    )
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, central_layout),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    assert [e.editor_id for e in result.editors("area.central")] == [
        "editor.mpr_axial",
        "editor.mpr_sagittal",
        "editor.mpr_coronal",
    ]


# ----- BuildResult accessors ----------------------------------------


def test_build_result_area_accessor(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    result = builder.build(spec)

    area = result.area("area.central")
    assert area.area_id == "area.central"
    assert area.placement is Placement.CENTRAL


def test_build_result_area_accessor_unknown_raises(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    result = builder.build(spec)

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        result.area("area.unknown")


def test_build_result_editors_accessor_unknown_raises(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    result = builder.build(spec)

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="not registered"):
        result.editors("area.unknown")


# ----- Errors -------------------------------------------------------


def test_build_unknown_editor_id_raises_key_error(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.does_not_exist")),
    )

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match=re.escape("editor.does_not_exist")):
        builder.build(spec)


def test_build_unknown_editor_id_in_nested_split_raises(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    central_layout = EditorLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (
            _leaf("editor.mpr_axial"),
            _leaf("editor.does_not_exist"),
        ),
    )
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, central_layout),
    )

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match=re.escape("editor.does_not_exist")):
        builder.build(spec)


def test_build_module_ui_spec_without_central_raises_at_spec_construction(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """ModuleUISpec itself rejects the spec, before the builder sees it.

    This test documents the layering: the invariant "at least one
    CENTRAL" is enforced by ModuleUISpec, not by the LayoutBuilder.
    """

    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="CENTRAL"):
        _spec(
            _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
            _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
        )


# ----- context_extras (hybrid EditorContext) ------------------------


def test_build_propagates_extras_to_each_editor(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area(
            "area.central",
            Placement.CENTRAL,
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
                (_leaf("editor.mpr_axial"), _leaf("editor.mpr_sagittal")),
            ),
        ),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec, context_extras={"project": "P-001"})

    editors = result.editors("area.central")
    assert len(editors) == 2
    for editor in editors:
        state = editor.persistent_state()
        assert state["extras"] == {"project": "P-001"}


def test_build_extras_are_copies_not_shared_references(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """Each Editor receives its own copy of the extras dict.

    Mutating one Editor's extras must not affect another's. This is
    the hybrid EditorContext property (ADR-0002 §2.1).
    """
    spec = _spec(
        _area(
            "area.central",
            Placement.CENTRAL,
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
                (_leaf("editor.mpr_axial"), _leaf("editor.mpr_sagittal")),
            ),
        ),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec, context_extras={"shared_key": "value"})

    a, b = result.editors("area.central")
    # Each Editor sees the same initial content...
    assert a.persistent_state()["extras"] == {"shared_key": "value"}
    assert b.persistent_state()["extras"] == {"shared_key": "value"}

    # ...but the two dicts are distinct objects.
    # We reach into the private attribute only in the test to prove
    # the invariant; production code never does this.
    extras_a = a.persistent_state()["extras"]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    extras_b = b.persistent_state()["extras"]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    assert extras_a is not extras_b


def test_build_without_extras_gives_empty_dicts(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    result = builder.build(spec)

    editor = result.editors("area.central")[0]
    assert editor.persistent_state()["extras"] == {}


def test_build_does_not_mutate_caller_extras(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """The builder must not mutate the caller's dict."""
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    original = {"project": "P-001"}
    snapshot = dict(original)

    builder.build(spec, context_extras=original)

    assert original == snapshot


# ----- shared_context (extension point, ADR-0002 §2.3) --------------


def test_build_accepts_shared_context_without_error(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """shared_context is accepted today as an extension point.

    It is not yet propagated, because EditorContext has no fields to
    receive shared services. This test documents that the parameter
    exists and does not break the build.
    """
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    # Passing a dict must not raise.
    result = builder.build(spec, shared_context={"scene_query": object()})
    assert len(result.areas) == 1


def test_build_rejects_non_mapping_context_extras(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="context_extras"):
        builder.build(spec, context_extras=["not", "a", "dict"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_build_rejects_non_mapping_shared_context(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="shared_context"):
        builder.build(spec, shared_context=["not", "a", "dict"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]


# ----- Builder reuse ------------------------------------------------


def test_builder_is_reusable_across_builds(builder: LayoutBuilder) -> None:
    """The builder carries no per-build state and can be reused."""
    spec_a = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    spec_b = _spec(
        _area(
            "area.central",
            Placement.CENTRAL,
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
                (_leaf("editor.mpr_axial"), _leaf("editor.mpr_sagittal")),
            ),
        ),
    )

    result_a = builder.build(spec_a)
    result_b = builder.build(spec_b)

    assert result_a.area_manager.area_count() == 1
    assert len(result_a.editors("area.central")) == 1
    assert result_b.area_manager.area_count() == 1
    assert len(result_b.editors("area.central")) == 2

    # The two results do not share state.
    assert result_a.area_manager is not result_b.area_manager
    assert result_a.tree is not result_b.tree


def test_builder_creates_distinct_editor_instances_per_build(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """Two builds of the same spec produce independent Editor instances."""
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )

    result_a = builder.build(spec)
    result_b = builder.build(spec)

    editor_a = result_a.editors("area.central")[0]
    editor_b = result_b.editors("area.central")[0]
    assert editor_a is not editor_b


def test_area_rejects_duplicate_editor_id_in_same_build(
    builder: LayoutBuilder,
) -> None:
    """The Area rejects two Editors with the same editor_id.

    Each editor_id within an Area must be unique. A user who wants
    two Viewport3D Editors in the same Area must register them under
    distinct editor_ids (e.g. editor.viewport_3d.left and
    editor.viewport_3d.right) pointing to the same Editor class.

    The invariant is enforced by Area.add_editor, not by the builder.
    The builder propagates the error without swallowing it.
    """
    spec = _spec(
        _area(
            "area.central",
            Placement.CENTRAL,
            EditorLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
                (_leaf("editor.viewport_3d"), _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
            ),
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(AreaError, match="already hosted"):
        builder.build(spec)



======================================================================
FILE: tests/integration/ui/workspace/layout/test_layout_renderer.py
======================================================================

"""Integration tests for LayoutRenderer.

The LayoutRenderer materializes a LayoutTree into Qt widgets. It
delegates the interior of each Area to an AreaWidget, and creates a
QSplitter per Split node.

These tests use qtbot: the renderer creates real widgets, and the
Editors it indirectly materializes (DummyEditor) return a QLabel as
content. A QApplication is therefore required.

What these tests cover:

- Single Leaf: render() returns an AreaWidget.
- Horizontal Split: render() returns a QSplitter with
  Qt.Horizontal orientation.
- Vertical Split: render() returns a QSplitter with Qt.Vertical.
- Nested splits: render() produces nested QSplitters.
- The four-area layout (LEFT + CENTRAL + RIGHT + BOTTOM): the root
  is a vertical split; the top row is a horizontal split.
- AreaWidget caching: rendering the same tree twice returns the
  same AreaWidget instances.
- cached_area_widget() exposes the AreaWidget by area_id.
- clear_cache() discards widgets.
- Empty tree (root is not a Leaf or Split): raises a clear error.
- Child order is preserved and matches the tree.
- Re-rendering after cache clear creates fresh widgets.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QSplitter

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_registry import EditorRegistry
from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeName, ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.toggle_tool import ToggleTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import ToolRegistry
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.area_widget import AreaWidget
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_builder import LayoutBuilder
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_renderer import (
    LayoutRenderer,
    LayoutRendererError,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.layout.layout_tree import Leaf
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import (
    EditorLayoutSpec,
)
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.module_ui_spec import ModuleUISpec

# ----- Local helpers ------------------------------------------------


def _leaf(editor_id: str) -> EditorLayoutSpec:
    return EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id)


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement,
    editor_layout: EditorLayoutSpec,
) -> AreaSpec:
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=editor_layout,
    )


def _spec(*areas: AreaSpec) -> ModuleUISpec:
    return ModuleUISpec(areas=tuple(areas))


def _build_result(*areas: AreaSpec):
    """Build a BuildResult from a set of AreaSpecs."""
    registry = EditorRegistry()
    for editor_id in (
        "editor.patient",
        "editor.viewport_3d",
        "editor.mpr_axial",
        "editor.mpr_sagittal",
        "editor.mpr_coronal",
        "editor.object_list",
        "editor.properties",
        "editor.console",
    ):
        registry.register(editor_id, DummyEditor)

    builder = LayoutBuilder(registry)
    return builder.build(_spec(*areas))


# ----- Fixtures local to this module --------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register("tool.log", LogTool)
    registry.register("tool.toggle", ToggleTool)
    return registry


@pytest.fixture
def theme_service() -> ThemeService:
    return ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.DARK)


@pytest.fixture
def renderer(
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
) -> LayoutRenderer:
    return LayoutRenderer(tool_registry, theme_service)


# ----- Single Leaf --------------------------------------------------


def test_render_single_leaf_returns_area_widget(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, AreaWidget)
    assert root.area_id == "area.central"


def test_render_leaf_tree_root_is_leaf(
    renderer: LayoutRenderer,
) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    assert isinstance(result.tree.root, Leaf)


# ----- Horizontal Split ---------------------------------------------


def test_render_horizontal_split_returns_qsplitter(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Orientation.Horizontal
    assert root.count() == 3


def test_render_horizontal_split_children_order(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    child_ids = []
    for i in range(root.count()):
        widget = root.widget(i)
        assert isinstance(widget, AreaWidget)
        child_ids.append(widget.area_id)
    assert child_ids == ["area.left", "area.central", "area.right"]


# ----- Vertical Split -----------------------------------------------


def test_render_vertical_split_returns_qsplitter(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, _leaf("editor.console")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Orientation.Vertical
    assert root.count() == 2


# ----- Nested Splits ------------------------------------------------


def test_render_four_areas_has_nested_splitters(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, _leaf("editor.properties")),
        _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, _leaf("editor.console")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    # Root: VERTICAL
    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Orientation.Vertical
    assert root.count() == 2

    # Top child: HORIZONTAL with 3 areas
    top = root.widget(0)
    assert isinstance(top, QSplitter)
    assert top.orientation() == Qt.Orientation.Horizontal
    assert top.count() == 3

    # Bottom child: single AreaWidget
    bottom = root.widget(1)
    assert isinstance(bottom, AreaWidget)
    assert bottom.area_id == "area.bottom"


# ----- Caching ------------------------------------------------------


def test_render_reuses_area_widgets_from_cache(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    root_first = renderer.render(result.tree)
    root_second = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root_first)

    assert root_first is root_second


def test_cached_area_widget_returns_widget(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, _leaf("editor.object_list")),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.viewport_3d")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    left = renderer.cached_area_widget("area.left")
    central = renderer.cached_area_widget("area.central")
    unknown = renderer.cached_area_widget("area.unknown")

    assert isinstance(left, AreaWidget)
    assert isinstance(central, AreaWidget)
    assert left.area_id == "area.left"
    assert central.area_id == "area.central"
    assert unknown is None


def test_clear_cache_discards_widgets(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    renderer.render(result.tree)

    renderer.clear_cache()

    assert renderer.cached_area_widget("area.central") is None


def test_render_after_clear_cache_creates_fresh_widgets(qtbot, renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    result = _build_result(
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, _leaf("editor.patient")),
    )
    first = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(first)

    renderer.clear_cache()
    second = renderer.render(result.tree)
    qtbot.addWidget(second)

    assert first is not second
    assert isinstance(first, AreaWidget)
    assert isinstance(second, AreaWidget)
    assert first.area_id == second.area_id


# ----- Errors -------------------------------------------------------


def test_render_invalid_node_raises(renderer: LayoutRenderer) -> None:
    class FakeTree:
        root = "not a node"

    with pytest.raises(LayoutRendererError, match="unknown layout node"):
        renderer.render(FakeTree())  # type: ignore[arg-type]



======================================================================
FILE: tests/integration/ui/workspace/workspace/test_workspace.py
======================================================================

"""Integration tests for the Workspace facade.

The Workspace is the highest-level component of the Workspace
subsystem. It coordinates:

- LayoutBuilder      (ModuleUISpec -> Areas, LayoutTree, Editors)
- LayoutRenderer     (LayoutTree -> Qt widgets)
- OverlayManager     (registration + repositioning of Overlays)
- TopOverlay         (the shared Top Bar, registered by default)

These tests exercise the full materialization path:

    ModuleUISpec
        -> Workspace.load_spec()
        -> Areas materialized, widgets rendered, overlays repositioned

They use qtbot and a real QApplication. Widgets are laid out by Qt
when the Workspace is shown; the tests call ``show()`` and
``qtbot.waitExposed()`` (or explicitly trigger a resize) when the
geometry matters.

The Workspace receives a ProjectContext at construction (ADR-0006).
A minimal fixture provides one for these tests; the context is not
inspected by the Workspace beyond its presence.

What these tests cover:

- Construction with the required registries, services, and context.
- The TopOverlay is registered by default and accessible.
- load_spec() builds the Areas, the tree, and the widget hierarchy.
- areas() returns the Areas of the current composition.
- area_manager() returns the manager; None before load.
- is_loaded() reflects the composition state.
- clear() drops the composition; the TopOverlay survives.
- Reloading with a different spec replaces the composition.
- Overlays are repositioned on load and on resize.
- Region bounds are derived from the rendered AreaWidgets.
- Empty workspace (no load) does not crash on resize.

Non-goals:

- Layout persistence (Bloco 11).
- Drag-and-drop, floating, multi-monitor (Bloco 14).
- Top Bar content (the three segments are populated by the
  MainWindow, not by the Workspace — ADR-0006).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.project.project_context import ProjectContext
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_registry import EditorRegistry
from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeName, ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.log_tool import LogTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.builtin.toggle_tool import ToggleTool
from cranioz.ui.tools.registry.tool_registry import ToolRegistry
from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import EditorLayoutSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.module_ui_spec import ModuleUISpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.workspace import Workspace

# ----- Local helpers ------------------------------------------------


def _leaf(editor_id: str) -> EditorLayoutSpec:
    return EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id)


def _area(
    area_id: str,
    placement: Placement,
    editor_id: str,
) -> AreaSpec:
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=_leaf(editor_id),
    )


def _spec(*areas: AreaSpec) -> ModuleUISpec:
    return ModuleUISpec(areas=tuple(areas))


# ----- Fixtures local to this module --------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def editor_registry() -> EditorRegistry:
    reg = EditorRegistry()
    for editor_id in (
        "editor.patient",
        "editor.viewport_3d",
        "editor.object_list",
        "editor.properties",
        "editor.console",
    ):
        reg.register(editor_id, DummyEditor)
    return reg


@pytest.fixture
def tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
    reg = ToolRegistry()
    reg.register("tool.log", LogTool)
    reg.register("tool.toggle", ToggleTool)
    return reg


@pytest.fixture
def theme_service() -> ThemeService:
    return ThemeService(theme=ThemeName.DARK)


@pytest.fixture
def project_context() -> ProjectContext:
    """A minimal ProjectContext for Workspace tests.

    The Workspace only checks for the presence of the context; it does
    not inspect its fields. This fixture provides a coherent one so
    that tests can construct a Workspace without a ProjectManager.
    """
    pid = ProjectId("p-001")
    return ProjectContext(
        project_id=pid,
        metadata=ProjectMetadata(
            project_id=pid,
            project_name="Case 001",
            patient_id="pat-001",
            patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
            clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
            status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
            created_at=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
            updated_at=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
            last_opened_at=None,
            schema_version=1,
        ),
        patient_summary=PatientSummary(
            patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
            display_name="Gabriel Silva",
            birth_date=None,
            sex=PatientSex.MALE,
        ),
    )


@pytest.fixture
def workspace(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    editor_registry: EditorRegistry,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    project_context: ProjectContext,
) -> Workspace:
    ws = Workspace(
        editor_registry=editor_registry,
        tool_registry=tool_registry,
        theme_service=theme_service,
        project_context=project_context,
    )
    qtbot.addWidget(ws)
    return ws


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_workspace_is_a_qwidget(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    assert isinstance(workspace, QWidget)


def test_workspace_has_object_name(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    assert workspace.objectName() == "workspace"


def test_workspace_starts_unloaded(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is False
    assert workspace.areas() == ()
    assert workspace.area_manager() is None


def test_workspace_registers_top_overlay_by_default(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    overlay = workspace.top_overlay()
    assert overlay is not None
    assert overlay.overlay_id == "overlay.top_bar"
    assert workspace.overlay_manager().contains("overlay.top_bar")


def test_workspace_exposes_project_context(
    workspace: Workspace,
    project_context: ProjectContext,
) -> None:
    """ADR-0006: the Workspace exposes the ProjectContext it was created for."""
    assert workspace.project_context is project_context


# ----- load_spec — basic materialization ----------------------------


def test_load_spec_single_central(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    assert workspace.is_loaded() is True
    assert [a.area_id for a in workspace.areas()] == ["area.central"]


def test_load_spec_left_central_right(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.object_list"),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.viewport_3d"),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.properties"),
    )
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    ids = sorted(a.area_id for a in workspace.areas())
    assert ids == ["area.central", "area.left", "area.right"]


def test_load_spec_four_areas(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.object_list"),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.viewport_3d"),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.properties"),
        _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.console"),
    )
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    ids = sorted(a.area_id for a in workspace.areas())
    assert ids == [
        "area.bottom",
        "area.central",
        "area.left",
        "area.right",
    ]


def test_load_spec_populates_area_manager(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    manager = workspace.area_manager()
    assert manager is not None
    assert manager.contains("area.central")
    assert manager.has_central()


# ----- clear and reload ---------------------------------------------


def test_clear_drops_composition(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is True

    workspace.clear()

    assert workspace.is_loaded() is False
    assert workspace.areas() == ()
    assert workspace.area_manager() is None


def test_clear_on_unloaded_workspace_is_noop(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    workspace.clear()  # must not raise
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is False


def test_clear_preserves_top_overlay(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    overlay_before = workspace.top_overlay()

    workspace.clear()

    # The TopOverlay instance is stable; the OverlayManager still
    # holds it.
    assert workspace.top_overlay() is overlay_before
    assert workspace.overlay_manager().contains("overlay.top_bar")


def test_reload_replaces_composition(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec_a = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    spec_b = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.object_list"),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.viewport_3d"),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.properties"),
    )

    workspace.load_spec(spec_a)
    workspace.load_spec(spec_b)

    ids = sorted(a.area_id for a in workspace.areas())
    assert ids == ["area.central", "area.left", "area.right"]


def test_reload_keeps_top_overlay_registered(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))

    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    assert workspace.overlay_manager().contains("overlay.top_bar")


# ----- Overlays and regions -----------------------------------------


def test_load_spec_triggers_overlay_repositioning(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    """After a load + resize, the TopOverlay has non-zero geometry.

    The TopOverlay is anchored to the Central Region. If the Central
    Area has a non-zero size, the TopOverlay must have a non-zero
    width.
    """
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.resize(800, 600)
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)

    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    workspace.resize(800, 600)  # trigger a resizeEvent

    overlay_widget = workspace.top_overlay().widget()
    # The overlay must have received some bounds; width may vary with
    # Qt's layout timing, but the widget must be visible.
    assert overlay_widget.isVisible() is True


def test_workspace_resize_does_not_crash_unloaded(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)
    workspace.resize(500, 400)  # no load; must not raise


def test_top_overlay_is_above_root_widget(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    """The TopOverlay floats above the rendered composition.

    The TopOverlay widget is a child of the Workspace and is raised
    above the composition's root widget. It must not be reparented
    into the root widget.
    """
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.resize(800, 600)
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)

    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    # The TopOverlay's widget's parent is the Workspace itself.
    overlay_widget = workspace.top_overlay().widget()
    assert overlay_widget.parent() is workspace


# ----- Overlay manager stability ------------------------------------


def test_overlay_manager_is_stable_across_load_spec(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    manager_before = workspace.overlay_manager()
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    manager_after = workspace.overlay_manager()
    assert manager_before is manager_after


def test_overlay_manager_is_stable_across_clear(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    manager_before = workspace.overlay_manager()
    workspace.clear()
    manager_after = workspace.overlay_manager()
    assert manager_before is manager_after

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/test_event_bus.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EventBus."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus, EventBusError


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleEvent:
    value: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OtherEvent:
    value: int


def test_publish_with_no_subscribers_is_noop() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))  # must not raise


def test_subscribe_and_publish_receives_event() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=42))

    assert received == [SampleEvent(value=42)]


def test_subscribers_are_isolated_by_event_type() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received_sample: list[SampleEvent] = []
    received_other: list[OtherEvent] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received_sample.append)
    bus.subscribe(OtherEvent, received_other.append)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert received_sample == [SampleEvent(value=1)]
    assert received_other == []


def test_multiple_subscribers_called_in_registration_order() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    order: list[str] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("first"))
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("second"))
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("third"))

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert order == ["first", "second", "third"]


def test_unsubscribe_removes_handler() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    handler = received.append
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, handler)
    bus.unsubscribe(SampleEvent, handler)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert received == []


def test_unsubscribe_unknown_handler_is_noop() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.unsubscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)  # must not raise


def test_failing_subscriber_does_not_block_others() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    called: list[str] = []

    def failing(_: SampleEvent) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, failing)
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: called.append("ok"))

    with pytest.raises(EventBusError):
        bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert called == ["ok"]


def test_subscriber_count() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    assert bus.subscriber_count(SampleEvent) == 0
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    assert bus.subscriber_count(SampleEvent) == 1
    assert bus.subscriber_count(OtherEvent) == 0



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_filesystem_project_repository.py
======================================================================

"""FilesystemProjectRepository — contract + filesystem-specific tests.

This file runs the *same contract* defined in Peça 7.1 against the
filesystem adapter. If the adapter honors the contract, the same test
bodies that pass for `InMemoryProjectRepository` must pass here.

Additional tests cover filesystem-specific behavior:

- `create` leaves no partial directory when project.json cannot be
  written.
- `exists` is keyed on `project.json`, not the directory (so a stray
  directory without project.json is not a project).
- `delete` removes only `project.json`; other files in the directory
  are left untouched (they belong to other persistence layers).
- `list_metadata` skips corrupted entries (a malformed project.json
  in one directory does not break the listing).
- `list_ids` skips non-project directories.

Qt-free, I/O is real but confined to `tmp_path`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.project_errors import (
    ProjectAlreadyExists,
    ProjectCorrupted,
    ProjectNotFound,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_repository import ProjectRepository
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.filesystem_project_repository import (
    FilesystemProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.project_serialization import (
    CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    project_to_dict,
)

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    )


def _project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(project_id=pid, metadata=_metadata(project_id))


@pytest.fixture
def root(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Isolated projects root for the test."""
    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
    projects.mkdir()
    return projects


@pytest.fixture
def repo(root: Path) -> ProjectRepository:
    return FilesystemProjectRepository(projects_root=root)


# ====================================================================
# Contract — same bodies as Peça 7.1
# ====================================================================


def test_satisfies_protocol(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert isinstance(repo, ProjectRepository)


def test_new_repository_is_empty(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_metadata() == ()
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


def test_create_then_get_returns_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    project = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(project)
    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.project_id == project.project_id
    assert loaded.metadata == project.metadata


def test_create_then_exists_is_true(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_exists_false_for_missing_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-999")) is False


def test_get_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound) as exc_info:
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-999"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-999")


def test_create_duplicate_raises_project_already_exists(
        repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists) as exc_info:
        repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_create_duplicate_does_not_overwrite(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    first = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(first)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists):
        repo.create(_project("p-001"))

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata == first.metadata


def test_update_replaces_stored_instance(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    original = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(original)

    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=original.project_id,
        metadata=original.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later),
    )
    repo.update(updated)

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata.project_name == "Renamed"


def test_update_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.update(_project("p-999"))


def test_delete_removes_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is False
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_delete_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.delete(ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_list_metadata_returns_summaries(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    result = repo.list_metadata()
    assert len(result) == 2
    for item in result:
        assert isinstance(item, ProjectSummary)


def test_list_metadata_reflects_current_state(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    assert {s.project_id for s in repo.list_metadata()} == {
        ProjectId("p-001"),
        ProjectId("p-002"),
    }

    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert {s.project_id for s in repo.list_metadata()} == {ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_ids_matches_created_ids(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert set(repo.list_ids()) == {ProjectId("p-001"), ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_ids_empty_when_nothing_created(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


# ====================================================================
# Filesystem-specific behavior
# ====================================================================


def test_create_writes_project_json(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    project = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(project)

    project_json = root / "p-001" / "project.json"
    assert project_json.is_file()
    data = json.loads(project_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert data == project_to_dict(project)


def test_create_does_not_write_patient_record(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """patient_record.json belongs to the Patient persistence layer."""
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert not (root / "p-001" / "patient_record.json").exists()


def test_exists_is_false_for_directory_without_project_json(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """A stray directory is not a project.

    This can happen if a crash leaves a directory behind, or if
    another layer (e.g. patient persistence) created it first.
    """
    (root / "stray").mkdir()
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("stray")) is False


def test_delete_removes_only_project_json(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """Other files in the directory belong to other layers."""
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    # Simulate a patient record written by another layer.
    patient_record = root / "p-001" / "patient_record.json"
    patient_record.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")

    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert not (root / "p-001" / "project.json").exists()
    assert patient_record.is_file()  # untouched


def test_list_ids_skips_non_project_directories(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "stray").mkdir()  # no project.json

    assert repo.list_ids() == (ProjectId("p-001"),)


def test_list_ids_skips_non_directory_entries(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "file.txt").write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")

    assert repo.list_ids() == (ProjectId("p-001"),)


def test_list_ids_includes_all_project_directories(
    repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """Any directory with a project.json is a project, regardless of name.

    The `ProjectId` value object itself does not reject path-unsafe
    names; that validation belongs to `ProjectPathResolver` at the
    point of use. `list_ids` reports what is on disk.
    """
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("weird.name"))

    ids = set(repo.list_ids())
    assert ProjectId("p-001") in ids
    assert ProjectId("weird.name") in ids


def test_get_corrupted_json_raises_project_corrupted(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "p-001" / "project.json").write_text("{not-json", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_get_non_object_json_raises_project_corrupted(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "p-001" / "project.json").write_text("[1, 2, 3]", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_get_schema_mismatch_raises_project_corrupted(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    project_json = root / "p-001" / "project.json"
    data = json.loads(project_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data["schema_version"] = 99
    project_json.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_list_metadata_skips_corrupted_entries(
        repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """One corrupted project does not break the listing."""
