FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_access_policy.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectAccessPolicy and AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
    ProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId


def test_allow_all_satisfies_protocol() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert isinstance(policy, ProjectAccessPolicy)


def test_allow_all_permits_create() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_create("alice") is True


def test_allow_all_permits_open() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_open("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_permits_update() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_update("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_permits_delete() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_delete("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_ignores_actor() -> None:
    """The permissive policy is actor-agnostic."""
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_create("") is True
    assert policy.can_open("", ProjectId("p-001")) is True



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_catalog.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectCatalog.

The catalog is a thin read-only projection over a ProjectRepository.
Tests exercise it against InMemoryProjectRepository — pure Python, no
I/O, no Qt.

Coverage:

- `list_projects` delegates to `repository.list_metadata`.
- `get_project_summary` returns the summary when present, None when
  not. It does not raise `ProjectNotFound`.
- `search_projects` is case-insensitive substring over project name
  and patient display name. Empty query returns all.
- `count`, `is_empty`, `contains` reflect the repository state.
- The catalog does not cache: mutations to the repository are
  reflected on the next call.
- A corrupted entry in the repository is invisible to the catalog
  (because `list_metadata` skips corrupted entries).
- The catalog does not expose lifecycle methods (create, delete, ...).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_catalog import ProjectCatalog
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _project(
    project_id: str,
    *,
    project_name: str | None = None,
    patient_display_name: str = "Gabriel Silva",
) -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(
        project_id=pid,
        metadata=ProjectMetadata(
            project_id=pid,
            project_name=project_name or f"Case {project_id}",
            patient_id="pat-001",
            patient_display_name=patient_display_name,
            clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
            status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
            created_at=_now(),
            updated_at=_now(),
            last_opened_at=None,
            schema_version=1,
        ),
    )


@pytest.fixture
def repository() -> InMemoryProjectRepository:
    return InMemoryProjectRepository()


@pytest.fixture
def catalog(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> ProjectCatalog:
    return ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_catalog_holds_repository(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> None:
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)
    # Sanity: constructing does not raise; the catalog is usable.
    assert catalog.list_projects() == ()


# ----- list_projects ------------------------------------------------


def test_list_projects_empty(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.list_projects() == ()


def test_list_projects_returns_all(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.list_projects()
    assert len(result) == 2
    for item in result:
        assert isinstance(item, ProjectSummary)


def test_list_projects_delegates_to_repository(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """The catalog must not maintain its own list.

    If it did, mutations after the first call would not be visible.
    """
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert len(catalog.list_projects()) == 1

    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert len(catalog.list_projects()) == 2


def test_list_projects_reflects_deletion(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    repository.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    ids = {s.project_id for s in catalog.list_projects()}
    assert ids == {ProjectId("p-002")}


# ----- get_project_summary -----------------------------------------


def test_get_project_summary_returns_summary(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Alpha"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    summary = catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert summary is not None
    assert summary.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert summary.project_name == "Alpha"


def test_get_project_summary_returns_none_for_missing(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    """The catalog returns None, it does not raise."""
    assert catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-999")) is None


def test_get_project_summary_does_not_raise_for_missing(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    """Explicit: no exception escapes get_project_summary for a missing id."""
    try:
        catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-999"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        pytest.fail(f"unexpected exception: {exc!r}")


# ----- search_projects ---------------------------------------------


def test_search_empty_query_returns_all(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("")) == 2
    assert len(catalog.search_projects("   ")) == 2


def test_search_matches_project_name_case_insensitive(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Alpha Case"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002", project_name="Beta Case"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.search_projects("alpha")
    assert len(result) == 1
    assert result[0].project_name == "Alpha Case"

    result = catalog.search_projects("ALPHA")
    assert len(result) == 1


def test_search_matches_patient_display_name(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(
        _project("p-001", project_name="A", patient_display_name="Maria Souza")
    )
    repository.create(
        _project("p-002", project_name="B", patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva")
    )
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.search_projects("maria")
    assert len(result) == 1
    assert result[0].patient_display_name == "Maria Souza"


def test_search_substring_matches(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """Substring, not prefix. 'ognath' matches 'Orthognathic'."""
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Orthognathic Case"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("ognath")) == 1


def test_search_no_match_returns_empty(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    assert catalog.search_projects("zzz") == ()


def test_search_does_not_use_regex(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """A regex-looking query is treated literally.

    If the catalog used regex, '.' would match any character. Here it
    must match only a literal dot. The project name contains 'a.b',
    so 'a.b' matches but 'axb' does not.
    """
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="a.b"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("a.b")) == 1
    assert len(catalog.search_projects("axb")) == 0


# ----- count / is_empty / contains ---------------------------------


def test_count_reflects_repository(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    assert catalog.count() == 0
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.count() == 1
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert catalog.count() == 2


def test_is_empty_reflects_repository(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    assert catalog.is_empty() is True
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.is_empty() is False


def test_contains_true_for_existing(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_contains_false_for_missing(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-999")) is False


# ----- Corrupted entries -------------------------------------------


def test_corrupted_entries_are_invisible(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """The catalog relies on list_metadata, which skips corrupted entries."""
    repository.create(_project("p-ok"))
    repository.create(_project("p-bad"))
    repository._mark_corrupted(ProjectId("p-bad"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert catalog.count() == 1
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-ok")) is True
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-bad")) is False


# ----- No lifecycle methods ----------------------------------------


def test_catalog_has_no_create_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    """The catalog is read-only. It must not expose create()."""
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "create")


def test_catalog_has_no_update_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "update")


def test_catalog_has_no_delete_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "delete")


def test_catalog_has_no_open_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "open_project")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_context.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectContext and ObjectReference.

The ProjectContext is the immutable snapshot handed to the Workspace
when a project opens. These tests cover:

- Construction and required/optional fields.
- The metadata/project_id coherence invariant.
- The workflow_id/workflow_config coupling invariant.
- Immutability (frozen).
- Functional mutators (`with_workflow`, `with_object_references`).
- `has_workflow` helper.
- `ObjectReference` validation.

No Qt, no I/O, no repository.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.project_context import (
    ObjectReference,
    ProjectContext,
)
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _patient_summary() -> PatientSummary:
    return PatientSummary(
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        birth_date=None,
        sex=PatientSex.MALE,
    )


def _context(
    project_id: str = "p-001",
    *,
    workflow_id: str | None = None,
    workflow_config: dict | None = None,
    object_references: tuple[ObjectReference, ...] = (),
) -> ProjectContext:
    return ProjectContext(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        metadata=_metadata(project_id),
        patient_summary=_patient_summary(),
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        workflow_config=workflow_config,
        object_references=object_references,
    )


# ----- ObjectReference ---------------------------------------------


def test_object_reference_holds_fields() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(
        object_id="ct-001",
        kind="volume",
        display_name="CT Pre-op",
    )
    assert ref.object_id == "ct-001"
    assert ref.kind == "volume"
    assert ref.display_name == "CT Pre-op"


def test_object_reference_is_frozen() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="k", display_name="d")
    attr = "kind"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(ref, attr, "other")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_object_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="object_id"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="", kind="k", display_name="d")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_kind() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="kind"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="", display_name="d")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_display_name() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="display_name"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="k", display_name="")


# ----- ProjectContext construction ---------------------------------


def test_context_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="ct-001", kind="volume", display_name="CT")
    ctx = _context(object_references=(ref,))

    assert ctx.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert ctx.metadata.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert ctx.patient_summary.display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert ctx.workflow_id is None
    assert ctx.workflow_config is None
    assert ctx.object_references == (ref,)


def test_context_defaults_workflow_to_none() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert ctx.workflow_id is None
    assert ctx.workflow_config is None
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is False


def test_context_accepts_workflow_when_both_are_set() -> None:
    ctx = _context(
        workflow_id="orthognathic_planning",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["patient_data"]},
    )
    assert ctx.workflow_id == "orthognathic_planning"
    assert ctx.workflow_config == {"steps": ["patient_data"]}
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is True


def test_context_is_frozen() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(ctx, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_context_object_references_default_is_empty_tuple() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert ctx.object_references == ()


def test_context_object_references_default_is_immutable_tuple() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert isinstance(ctx.object_references, tuple)


# ----- Invariants ----------------------------------------------------


def test_metadata_project_id_mismatch_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"metadata\.project_id must match"):
        ProjectContext(
            project_id=ProjectId("p-001"),
            metadata=_metadata("p-999"),
            patient_summary=_patient_summary(),
        )


def test_workflow_id_without_config_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id and workflow_config"):
        _context(workflow_id="orthognathic", workflow_config=None)


def test_workflow_config_without_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id and workflow_config"):
        _context(workflow_id=None, workflow_config={"steps": []})


# ----- with_workflow ------------------------------------------------


def test_with_workflow_returns_new_context() -> None:
    original = _context()
    updated = original.with_workflow(
        workflow_id="orthognathic",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["a"]},
    )

    assert updated is not original
    assert updated.workflow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}
    assert original.workflow_id is None


def test_with_workflow_rejects_empty_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id"):
        _context().with_workflow(workflow_id="", workflow_config={})


def test_with_workflow_copies_config_dict() -> None:
    original = _context()
    source = {"steps": ["a"]}
    updated = original.with_workflow("wf", source)

    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}
    assert updated.workflow_config is not source


# ----- with_object_references ---------------------------------------


def test_with_object_references_returns_new_context() -> None:
    original = _context()
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="ct-001", kind="volume", display_name="CT")
    updated = original.with_object_references((ref,))

    assert updated is not original
    assert updated.object_references == (ref,)
    assert original.object_references == ()


def test_with_object_references_preserves_workflow() -> None:
    original = _context(
        workflow_id="orthognathic",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["a"]},
    )
    updated = original.with_object_references(())

    assert updated.workflow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}


# ----- has_workflow -------------------------------------------------


def test_has_workflow_false_when_none() -> None:
    assert _context().has_workflow() is False


def test_has_workflow_true_when_set() -> None:
    ctx = _context(workflow_id="wf", workflow_config={})
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is True



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_events.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Project lifecycle events.

Events are frozen data. These tests cover:

- Construction and required fields.
- Immutability (frozen).
- Equality by value.
- The presence of `project_id` on every event (the minimum contract
  every observer relies on).

The tests do not publish or subscribe; publication is the Project
Manager's concern (Peça 7.5).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.project_context import ProjectContext
from cranioz.application.project.project_events import (
    ProjectClosed,
    ProjectClosing,
    ProjectCreated,
    ProjectDeleted,
    ProjectOpened,
    ProjectUpdated,
)
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _patient_summary() -> PatientSummary:
    return PatientSummary(
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        birth_date=None,
        sex=PatientSex.MALE,
    )


def _context(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectContext:
    return ProjectContext(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        metadata=_metadata(project_id),
        patient_summary=_patient_summary(),
    )


# ----- ProjectCreated -----------------------------------------------


def test_project_created_holds_fields() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    event = ProjectCreated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=md)
    assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert event.metadata is md


def test_project_created_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectCreated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata())
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_project_created_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = ProjectCreated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata())
    b = ProjectCreated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata())
    assert a == b


# ----- ProjectOpened ------------------------------------------------


def test_project_opened_holds_context() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    event = ProjectOpened(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), context=ctx)
    assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert event.context is ctx


def test_project_opened_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectOpened(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), context=_context())
    attr = "context"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, _context())


# ----- ProjectUpdated -----------------------------------------------


def test_project_updated_holds_metadata() -> None:
    md = _metadata()
    event = ProjectUpdated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=md)
    assert event.metadata is md


def test_project_updated_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectUpdated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata())
    attr = "metadata"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, _metadata())


# ----- ProjectClosing / ProjectClosed -------------------------------


def test_project_closing_holds_only_id() -> None:
    event = ProjectClosing(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert len(dataclasses.fields(event)) == 1


def test_project_closed_holds_only_id() -> None:
    event = ProjectClosed(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert len(dataclasses.fields(event)) == 1


def test_project_closing_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectClosing(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_project_closed_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectClosed(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


# ----- ProjectDeleted -----------------------------------------------


def test_project_deleted_holds_only_id() -> None:
    event = ProjectDeleted(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert len(dataclasses.fields(event)) == 1


def test_project_deleted_is_frozen() -> None:
    event = ProjectDeleted(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"))
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(event, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


# ----- Cross-cutting -----------------------------------------------


def test_every_event_has_project_id() -> None:
    """Every lifecycle event carries the project it refers to.

    This is the minimum contract every observer relies on: from the
    event alone, an observer can route to the right project.
    """
    events = [
        ProjectCreated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata()),
        ProjectOpened(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), context=_context()),
        ProjectUpdated(project_id=ProjectId("p-001"), metadata=_metadata()),
        ProjectClosing(project_id=ProjectId("p-001")),
        ProjectClosed(project_id=ProjectId("p-001")),
        ProjectDeleted(project_id=ProjectId("p-001")),
    ]
    for event in events:
        assert hasattr(event, "project_id")
        assert event.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_manager.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectManager.

Exercises the full lifecycle against InMemoryProjectRepository, a real
EventBus, and AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy. No Qt, no I/O.

Coverage:

- create_project: persists via repository, publishes ProjectCreated,
  respects access policy, rejects duplicates via repository.
- open_project: builds ProjectContext from project metadata,
  updates last_opened_at, publishes ProjectOpened once, returns the
  existing context on repeated open (idempotent), honors workflow_id
  and workflow_config, respects access policy.
- open_project PatientSummary: display_name and patient_id derived
  from project metadata; birth_date and sex are None (not carried).
- update_project: persists and publishes ProjectUpdated; respects
  access policy; no event on repository failure.
- close_project: publishes ProjectClosing then ProjectClosed, releases
  the context; silent no-op for non-open project.
- delete_project: refuses open project with ProjectInUse BEFORE
  mutating state or publishing events; respects access policy.
- Access policy denial blocks every operation and publishes nothing.
- No events are published when the underlying repository operation
  fails.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus
from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    Actor,
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
    ProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_context import ProjectContext
from cranioz.application.project.project_errors import (
    ProjectAccessDenied,
    ProjectAlreadyExists,
    ProjectInUse,
    ProjectNotFound,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_events import (
    ProjectClosed,
    ProjectClosing,
    ProjectCreated,
    ProjectDeleted,
    ProjectOpened,
    ProjectUpdated,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_manager import ProjectManager
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


_ACTOR: Actor = "test-user"


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _make_project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(
        project_id=pid,
        metadata=ProjectMetadata(
            project_id=pid,
            project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
            patient_id="pat-001",
            patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
            clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
            status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
            created_at=_now(),
            updated_at=_now(),
            last_opened_at=None,
            schema_version=1,
        ),
    )


def _make_manager(
    *,
    policy: ProjectAccessPolicy | None = None,
    bus: EventBus | None = None,
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository | None = None,
) -> tuple[ProjectManager, InMemoryProjectRepository, EventBus]:
    repo = repository if repository is not None else InMemoryProjectRepository()
    event_bus = bus if bus is not None else EventBus()
    policy = policy if policy is not None else AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=event_bus,
        access_policy=policy,
    )
    return manager, repo, event_bus


def _create(
    manager: ProjectManager,
    project_id: str = "p-001",
    *,
    project_name: str = "Case 001",
    patient_display_name: str = "Gabriel Silva",
) -> Project:
    return manager.create_project(
        _ACTOR,
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=project_name,
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        patient_display_name=patient_display_name,
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
    )


class _DenyAllPolicy:
    """Deny-everything policy for negative tests."""

    def can_create(self, actor: Actor) -> bool:
        return False

    def can_open(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return False

    def can_update(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return False

    def can_delete(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return False


class _DenyDeletePolicy:
    """Deny only deletes; allow the rest."""

    def can_create(self, actor: Actor) -> bool:
        return True

    def can_open(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return True

    def can_update(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return True

    def can_delete(self, actor: Actor, project_id: ProjectId) -> bool:
        return False


# ====================================================================
# create_project
# ====================================================================


def test_create_project_persists() -> None:
    manager, repo, _ = _make_manager()
    project = _create(manager, "p-001")

    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True
    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata.project_name == project.metadata.project_name


def test_create_project_publishes_created_event() -> None:
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    received: list[ProjectCreated] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectCreated, received.append)

    _create(manager, "p-001")

    assert len(received) == 1
    assert received[0].project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_create_project_returns_project() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    project = _create(manager, "p-001", project_name="Custom")
    assert project.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert project.metadata.project_name == "Custom"


def test_create_project_defaults_to_draft_status() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    project = _create(manager, "p-001")
    assert project.metadata.status is ProjectStatus.DRAFT


def test_create_project_denied_by_policy() -> None:
    manager, repo, bus = _make_manager(policy=_DenyAllPolicy())
    received: list[ProjectCreated] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectCreated, received.append)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAccessDenied):
        _create(manager)

    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is False
    assert received == []


def test_create_project_duplicate_raises_from_repository() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists):
        _create(manager, "p-001")


def test_create_project_does_not_publish_on_repository_failure() -> None:
    """If the repository raises (here: duplicate), no event is published."""
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    received: list[ProjectCreated] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectCreated, received.append)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists):
        _create(manager, "p-001")

    assert received == []


# ====================================================================
# open_project
# ====================================================================


def test_open_project_returns_context() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    context = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert isinstance(context, ProjectContext)
    assert context.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_open_project_patient_summary_is_projected_from_metadata() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001", patient_display_name="Maria Souza")

    context = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert context.patient_summary.display_name == "Maria Souza"
    assert context.patient_summary.patient_id == PatientId("pat-001")
    # Not carried by the project; explicitly None.
    assert context.patient_summary.birth_date is None
    assert context.patient_summary.sex is None


def test_open_project_updates_last_opened() -> None:
    manager, repo, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    before = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")).metadata.last_opened_at
    assert before is None

    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    after = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")).metadata.last_opened_at
    assert after is not None


def test_open_project_publishes_opened_event() -> None:
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    received: list[ProjectOpened] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectOpened, received.append)

    context = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert len(received) == 1
    assert received[0].project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert received[0].context is context


def test_open_project_is_idempotent() -> None:
    """Opening an already-open project returns the existing context."""
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    first = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    second = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert first is second
    # Only one event total (subscribed after the first open).
    received: list[ProjectOpened] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectOpened, received.append)
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert received == []


def test_open_project_with_workflow_propagates_to_context() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    context = manager.open_project(
        _ACTOR,
        ProjectId("p-001"),
        workflow_id="orthognathic",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["a"]},
    )

    assert context.workflow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert context.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}


def test_open_project_without_workflow() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    context = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert context.workflow_id is None
    assert context.workflow_config is None


def test_open_project_denied_by_policy() -> None:
    """Deny the open; do not touch state or publish events."""
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    repo.create(_make_project("p-001"))
    bus = EventBus()
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=bus,
        access_policy=_DenyAllPolicy(),
    )

    received: list[ProjectOpened] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectOpened, received.append)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAccessDenied):
        manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is False
    assert received == []


def test_open_project_missing_raises() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-999"))


# ====================================================================
# update_project
# ====================================================================


def test_update_project_persists() -> None:
    manager, repo, _ = _make_manager()
    project = _create(manager, "p-001")

    later = project.metadata.created_at + timedelta(days=1)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=project.project_id,
        metadata=project.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later),
    )
    manager.update_project(_ACTOR, updated)

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata.project_name == "Renamed"


def test_update_project_publishes_updated_event() -> None:
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    project = _create(manager, "p-001")
    received: list[ProjectUpdated] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectUpdated, received.append)

    later = project.metadata.created_at + timedelta(days=1)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=project.project_id,
        metadata=project.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later),
    )
    manager.update_project(_ACTOR, updated)

    assert len(received) == 1
    assert received[0].project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert received[0].metadata.project_name == "Renamed"


def test_update_project_denied_by_policy() -> None:
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    repo.create(_make_project("p-001"))
    bus = EventBus()
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=bus,
        access_policy=_DenyAllPolicy(),
    )

    received: list[ProjectUpdated] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectUpdated, received.append)

    project = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=project.project_id,
        metadata=project.metadata.with_name("X", now=later),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAccessDenied):
        manager.update_project(_ACTOR, updated)
    assert received == []


# ====================================================================
# close_project
# ====================================================================


def test_close_project_releases_context() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is False


def test_close_project_publishes_events_in_order() -> None:
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    order: list[str] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectClosing, lambda _e: order.append("closing"))
    bus.subscribe(ProjectClosed, lambda _e: order.append("closed"))

    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert order == ["closing", "closed"]


def test_close_project_does_not_modify_persisted_metadata() -> None:
    """last_opened_at is set on open; close does not update it."""
    manager, repo, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    before = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")).metadata

    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    after = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")).metadata
    assert after == before


def test_close_project_non_open_is_noop() -> None:
    """Closing a project that is not open does nothing and publishes nothing."""
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    # Not opened.

    received: list[object] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectClosing, received.append)
    bus.subscribe(ProjectClosed, received.append)

    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))  # must not raise

    assert received == []
    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is False


# ====================================================================
# delete_project
# ====================================================================


def test_delete_project_removes_from_repository() -> None:
    manager, repo, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")

    manager.delete_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is False


def test_delete_project_publishes_event() -> None:
    manager, _, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    received: list[ProjectDeleted] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectDeleted, received.append)

    manager.delete_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert len(received) == 1
    assert received[0].project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_delete_project_rejects_open_project() -> None:
    """Deleting an open project raises ProjectInUse BEFORE mutating state."""
    manager, repo, bus = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    received: list[ProjectDeleted] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectDeleted, received.append)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectInUse, match="close it first"):
        manager.delete_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    # State was not modified.
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True
    # No event was published.
    assert received == []


def test_delete_project_denied_by_policy() -> None:
    """Deny the delete; do not remove from repository, do not publish."""
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    repo.create(_make_project("p-001"))
    bus = EventBus()
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=bus,
        access_policy=_DenyDeletePolicy(),
    )

    received: list[ProjectDeleted] = []
    bus.subscribe(ProjectDeleted, received.append)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAccessDenied):
        manager.delete_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True
    assert received == []


def test_delete_project_missing_raises() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        manager.delete_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-999"))

    _create(manager, "p-001")
    context = manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert manager.get_active_context(ProjectId("p-001")) is context


def test_get_active_context_returns_none_after_close() -> None:
    manager, _, _ = _make_manager()
    _create(manager, "p-001")
    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert manager.get_active_context(ProjectId("p-001")) is None

def test_is_open_false_before_open() -> None:

    _create(manager, "p-001")
    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is False


def test_is_open_true_after_open() -> None:

    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is True

    manager.open_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    manager.close_project(_ACTOR, ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert manager.is_open(ProjectId("p-001")) is False



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_repository_contract.py
======================================================================

"""Contract tests for ProjectRepository.

These tests define the expected behavior of *any* ProjectRepository
implementation. The same suite is run against:

- `InMemoryProjectRepository` (this file).
- `FilesystemProjectRepository` (Peça 7.3, in a separate file that
  imports the same fixtures and test bodies).

The strategy is the classic "contract test" pattern: the test suite
is the specification, and every implementation must pass it. If a new
implementation is added, it must be accompanied by its own parameterized
version of this file — not a re-implementation of the same tests.

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_errors import (
    ProjectAlreadyExists,
    ProjectCorrupted,
    ProjectNotFound,
    ProjectRepositoryError,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_repository import ProjectRepository
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary

# ----- Fixtures -----------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(project_id=pid, metadata=_metadata(project_id))


@pytest.fixture
def repo() -> ProjectRepository:
    """Fresh repository per test."""
    return InMemoryProjectRepository()


# ----- Sanity --------------------------------------------------------


def test_in_memory_repository_satisfies_protocol(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    """Sanity: the in-memory repository satisfies the Protocol at runtime."""
    assert isinstance(repo, ProjectRepository)


def test_new_repository_is_empty(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_metadata() == ()
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


# ----- create / get round-trip ---------------------------------------


def test_create_then_get_returns_same_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    project = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(project)

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded is project


def test_create_then_exists_is_true(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_exists_false_for_missing_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-999")) is False


def test_get_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound) as exc_info:
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-999"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-999")
    assert "p-999" in str(exc_info.value)


def test_create_duplicate_raises_project_already_exists(
    repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists) as exc_info:
        repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_create_duplicate_does_not_overwrite(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    first = _project("p-001")
    second = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(first)

    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists):
        repo.create(second)

    # The original is untouched.
    assert repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")) is first


# ----- update --------------------------------------------------------


def test_update_replaces_stored_instance(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    original = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(original)

    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=original.project_id,
        metadata=original.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later),
    )
    repo.update(updated)

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded is updated
    assert loaded.metadata.project_name == "Renamed"


def test_update_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.update(_project("p-999"))


# ----- delete --------------------------------------------------------


def test_delete_removes_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is False
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_delete_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    """The contract requires explicit ProjectNotFound, not a silent no-op.

    Rationale (project_repository.py docstring): the Project Manager
    needs to distinguish "deleted now" from "already gone" to publish
    the right lifecycle event. Idempotent callers check `exists` first.
    """
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.delete(ProjectId("p-999"))


# ----- list_metadata -------------------------------------------------


def test_list_metadata_returns_summaries_not_projects(
    repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    result = repo.list_metadata()

    assert len(result) == 2
    for item in result:
        assert isinstance(item, ProjectSummary)
        # Sanity: no Project leaks through.
        assert not isinstance(item, Project)


def test_list_metadata_reflects_current_state(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    ids = {summary.project_id for summary in repo.list_metadata()}
    assert ids == {ProjectId("p-001"), ProjectId("p-002")}

    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    ids = {summary.project_id for summary in repo.list_metadata()}
    assert ids == {ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_metadata_order_is_unspecified(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    """The contract documents that order is not guaranteed.

    This test documents that the returned order matches insertion for
    the current in-memory implementation, but it explicitly checks the
    *set*, not the *sequence*, so that a future implementation may
    legitimately return a different order.
    """
    repo.create(_project("p-b"))
    repo.create(_project("p-a"))

    summaries = repo.list_metadata()
    assert {s.project_id for s in summaries} == {
        ProjectId("p-a"),
        ProjectId("p-b"),
    }


# ----- list_ids ------------------------------------------------------


def test_list_ids_matches_created_ids(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    ids = set(repo.list_ids())
    assert ids == {ProjectId("p-001"), ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_ids_empty_when_nothing_created(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


# ----- error hierarchy -----------------------------------------------


def test_all_repository_errors_share_a_base() -> None:
    """Every repository error derives from ProjectRepositoryError.

    This lets the Project Manager catch the whole family with one
    `except ProjectRepositoryError:` and still discriminate more
    specific failures by subclass.
    """
    for cls in (
        ProjectNotFound,
        ProjectAlreadyExists,
        ProjectCorrupted,
    ):
        assert issubclass(cls, ProjectRepositoryError)


def test_project_not_found_is_not_a_key_error() -> None:
    """The error is domain-aware; it must not be caught by `except KeyError`.

    The repository uses dicts internally, so a naive implementation
    might raise `KeyError`. The contract forbids this. This test
    documents the boundary.
    """
    assert not issubclass(ProjectNotFound, KeyError)


# ----- ProjectCorrupted (test hook) ----------------------------------


def test_marked_corrupted_project_raises_on_get(
    repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """The in-memory repository supports a test-only corruption hook.

    This lets tests exercise the `ProjectCorrupted` path without a
    filesystem. The FilesystemProjectRepository (Peça 7.3) will
    produce the same error through a real corrupted file.
    """
    assert isinstance(repo, InMemoryProjectRepository)
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo._mark_corrupted(ProjectId("p-001"))

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted) as exc_info:
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_list_metadata_skips_corrupted_entries(
    repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """A corrupted project does not hide the others from the listing.

    The read model is resilient: `list_metadata` returns what it can
    read. A corrupt entry is the Project Manager's problem to surface
    (Peça 7.5), not something that should crash the Home Page.
    """
    assert isinstance(repo, InMemoryProjectRepository)
    repo.create(_project("p-ok"))
    repo.create(_project("p-bad"))
    repo._mark_corrupted(ProjectId("p-bad"))

    summaries = repo.list_metadata()
    assert {s.project_id for s in summaries} == {ProjectId("p-ok")}


def test_delete_clears_corruption_marker(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    """Deleting a corrupted project removes the marker too.

    Otherwise `_corrupted` would grow unbounded across a long test
    session. The set is an implementation detail, but its cleanup is
    observable through behavior: after delete, recreating the id must
    succeed and `get` must return the new project.
    """
    assert isinstance(repo, InMemoryProjectRepository)
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo._mark_corrupted(ProjectId("p-001"))
    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))

    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert repo.get(ProjectId("p-001")).project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


# ----- Type narrowing ------------------------------------------------


def test_repository_error_factory_helpers() -> None:
    """Sanity: constructing each error produces a usable instance.

    Ensures the `project_id` attribute is always present, so logs can
    rely on it without `getattr(..., None)` gymnastics.
    """
    not_found = ProjectNotFound("x", project_id=ProjectId("p-1"))
    assert not_found.project_id == ProjectId("p-1")

    already = ProjectAlreadyExists("x", project_id=ProjectId("p-2"))
    assert already.project_id == ProjectId("p-2")

    corrupted = ProjectCorrupted("x", project_id=ProjectId("p-3"))
    assert corrupted.project_id == ProjectId("p-3")

    # The attribute is optional.
    no_id = ProjectNotFound("x")
    assert no_id.project_id is None



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_serialization.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Project serialization.

Pure functions, no I/O.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.project_serialization import (
    CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    ProjectSerializationError,
    project_from_dict,
    project_to_dict,
)


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(
        project_id=pid,
        metadata=ProjectMetadata(
            project_id=pid,
            project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
            patient_id="pat-001",
            patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
            clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
            status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
            created_at=_now(),
            updated_at=_now(),
            last_opened_at=None,
            schema_version=CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        ),
    )


# ----- Round-trip ----------------------------------------------------


def test_round_trip_preserves_all_fields() -> None:
    original = _project("p-001")
    reconstructed = project_from_dict(project_to_dict(original))
    assert reconstructed.project_id == original.project_id
    assert reconstructed.metadata == original.metadata


def test_round_trip_preserves_last_opened() -> None:
    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, 15, 30, tzinfo=UTC)
    original = _project("p-001")
    original = Project(
        project_id=original.project_id,
        metadata=original.metadata.with_last_opened(now=later),
    )
    reconstructed = project_from_dict(project_to_dict(original))
    assert reconstructed.metadata.last_opened_at == later


# ----- Serialization shape -------------------------------------------


def test_serialized_dict_contains_expected_keys() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    expected = {
        "schema_version",
        "project_id",
        "project_name",
        "patient_id",
        "patient_display_name",
        "clinical_procedure",
        "status",
        "created_at",
        "updated_at",
        "last_opened_at",
    }
    assert set(data.keys()) == expected


def test_status_is_serialized_by_value() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    assert data["status"] == "active"


def test_timestamps_are_iso_with_timezone() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    assert data["created_at"].endswith("+00:00")
    assert data["updated_at"].endswith("+00:00")


def test_last_opened_none_is_serialized_as_null() -> None:
    assert project_to_dict(_project())["last_opened_at"] is None


# ----- Decode errors --------------------------------------------------


def test_missing_required_key_raises() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    del data["project_name"]
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="project_name"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_invalid_schema_version_raises() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["schema_version"] = 99
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="schema_version"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_invalid_status_raises() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["status"] = "not-a-status"
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="status"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_invalid_timestamp_raises() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["created_at"] = "not-a-date"
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="created_at"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_naive_timestamp_raises() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["created_at"] = "2026-01-01T12:00:00"
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="timezone-aware"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_bool_schema_version_is_rejected() -> None:
    """bool is a subclass of int; the parser must reject it explicitly."""
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["schema_version"] = True
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="schema_version"):
        project_from_dict(data)


def test_non_dict_input_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="dict"):
        project_from_dict([])  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_domain_invariant_failure_is_wrapped() -> None:
    data = project_to_dict(_project())
    data["updated_at"] = "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"
    with pytest.raises(ProjectSerializationError, match="updated_at"):
        project_from_dict(data)

"""Tests for ConfigurationLoader."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from cranioz.config.loader import ConfigurationLoader
from cranioz.config.schema import ConfigError


@pytest.fixture
def defaults_dir(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Temporary directory with minimal valid configs."""
    (tmp_path / "app.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "app": {
                    "name": "CranioZ",
                    "version": "0.1.0",
                    "language": "pt-BR-BR",
                    "theme": "dark",
                    "recent_projects_limit": 10,
                },
                "logging": {
                    "level": "INFO",
                    "format": "%(message)s",
                },
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (tmp_path / "paths.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "paths": {
                    "project_root": None,
                    "flows_root": None,
                    "cache_root": None,
                }
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return tmp_path


def test_load_returns_config(defaults_dir: Path) -> None:
    cfg = ConfigurationLoader(defaults_dir).load()
    assert cfg.app.name == "CranioZ"
    assert cfg.app.language == "pt-BR-BR"
    assert cfg.app.theme == "dark"
    assert cfg.app.recent_projects_limit == 10
    assert cfg.logging.level == "INFO"
    assert cfg.paths.project_root is None


def test_missing_app_json_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "paths.json").write_text(json.dumps({"paths": {}}), encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match=re.escape("app.json")):
        ConfigurationLoader(tmp_path).load()


def test_missing_paths_json_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "app.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "app": {
                    "name": "CranioZ",
                    "version": "0.1.0",
                    "language": "pt-BR-BR",
                    "theme": "dark",
                    "recent_projects_limit": 10,
                },
                "logging": {"level": "INFO", "format": "%(message)s"},
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match=re.escape("paths.json")):
        ConfigurationLoader(tmp_path).load()


def test_invalid_json_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "app.json").write_text("{not json", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "paths.json").write_text(json.dumps({"paths": {}}), encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="invalid JSON"):
        ConfigurationLoader(tmp_path).load()


def test_missing_app_section_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "app.json").write_text(json.dumps({}), encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "paths.json").write_text(json.dumps({"paths": {}}), encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError, match="app"):
        ConfigurationLoader(tmp_path).load()

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/test_patient.py

"""Tests for Patient.

Patient is the aggregate root of the Patient domain. It carries the
PatientId, nine section value objects, and the cross-section
invariants. This file tests:

- Construction with the nine sections
- Equality and hashing by PatientId
- The women's health cross-section invariant
- Convenience methods: display_name, age, summary
- Representation

The tests do not depend on Qt, filesystem, or infrastructure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient import Patient
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.address import PatientAddress
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.antecedents import PatientAntecedents
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.comorbidities import (
    PatientComorbidities,
)
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.complaint import PatientComplaint
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.documents import PatientDocuments
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.filiation import PatientFiliation
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.habits import PatientHabits
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.identity import PatientIdentity
from cranioz.domain.patient.sections.medications import PatientMedications
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _patient(
    patient_id: str = "pat-001",
    *,
    full_name: str = "Gabriel Silva",
    social_name: str | None = None,
    birth_date: date | None = None,
    sex: PatientSex | None = None,
    pregnancy_contraception: str = "",
    breastfeeding: str = "",
) -> Patient:
    """Build a Patient with sensible defaults.

    Only the fields relevant to a given test are overridden via
    keyword arguments. Every section is present, even if empty.
    """
    return Patient(
        patient_id=PatientId(patient_id),
        identity=PatientIdentity(
            full_name=full_name,
            social_name=social_name,
            birth_date=birth_date,
            sex=sex,
        ),
        documents=PatientDocuments(),
        filiation=PatientFiliation(),
        address=PatientAddress(),
        complaint=PatientComplaint(),
        comorbidities=PatientComorbidities(),
        medications=PatientMedications(),
        antecedents=PatientAntecedents(
            pregnancy_contraception=pregnancy_contraception,
            breastfeeding=breastfeeding,
        ),
        habits=PatientHabits(),
    )


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_patient_holds_id_and_sections() -> None:
    patient = _patient()
    assert patient.patient_id == PatientId("pat-001")
    assert patient.identity.full_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert isinstance(patient.documents, PatientDocuments)
    assert isinstance(patient.antecedents, PatientAntecedents)


def test_patient_can_be_built_with_minimal_sections() -> None:
    patient = _patient()
    # Every section is a value object, even if empty.
    assert patient.documents.document_type is None
    assert patient.filiation.father_name is None
    assert patient.address.city is None
    assert patient.complaint.chief_complaint == ""
    assert patient.comorbidities.cardiovascular == ""
    assert patient.medications.allergies == ""
    assert patient.antecedents.previous_surgeries == ""
    assert patient.habits.smoking == ""


# ----- Equality and hashing ----------------------------------------


def test_two_patients_with_same_id_are_equal() -> None:
    a = _patient("pat-001", full_name="Gabriel Silva")
    b = _patient("pat-001", full_name="Gabriel S. Silva")
    # Different names, same id → same patient.
    assert a == b


def test_two_patients_with_different_ids_are_not_equal() -> None:
    a = _patient("pat-001")
    b = _patient("pat-002")
    assert a != b


def test_patient_equality_with_other_type_returns_false() -> None:
    patient = _patient()
    assert patient != "pat-001"
    assert patient != 42


def test_patient_hash_is_based_on_id() -> None:
    a = _patient("pat-001")
    b = _patient("pat-001")
    assert hash(a) == hash(b)


def test_patient_can_be_used_in_a_set() -> None:
    a = _patient("pat-001")
    b = _patient("pat-001")  # same id
    c = _patient("pat-002")
    patients = {a, b, c}
    assert len(patients) == 2


def test_patient_can_be_used_as_dict_key() -> None:
    a = _patient("pat-001")
    b = _patient("pat-001")
    d = {a: "first"}
    # b is equal to a, so it resolves the same entry.
    assert d[b] == "first"


# ----- Women's health cross-section invariant ----------------------


def test_female_patient_can_have_womens_health_data() -> None:
    patient = _patient(
        sex=PatientSex.FEMALE,
        pregnancy_contraception="Not pregnant",
        breastfeeding="No",
    )
    assert patient.antecedents.pregnancy_contraception == "Not pregnant"
    assert patient.antecedents.breastfeeding == "No"


def test_female_patient_with_empty_womens_health_data_is_valid() -> None:
    # The fields are optional even for female patients.
    patient = _patient(sex=PatientSex.FEMALE)
    assert patient.antecedents.pregnancy_contraception == ""
    assert patient.antecedents.breastfeeding == ""


def test_male_patient_with_pregnancy_contraception_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pregnancy_contraception"):
        _patient(
            sex=PatientSex.MALE,
            pregnancy_contraception="X",
        )


def test_male_patient_with_breastfeeding_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="breastfeeding"):
        _patient(
            sex=PatientSex.MALE,
            breastfeeding="X",
        )


def test_other_patient_with_pregnancy_contraception_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pregnancy_contraception"):
        _patient(
            sex=PatientSex.OTHER,
            pregnancy_contraception="X",
        )


def test_patient_with_sex_none_with_pregnancy_contraception_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pregnancy_contraception"):
        _patient(
            sex=None,
            pregnancy_contraception="X",
        )


def test_patient_with_sex_none_with_breastfeeding_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="breastfeeding"):
        _patient(
            sex=None,
            breastfeeding="X",
        )


def test_patient_with_sex_none_and_empty_womens_health_is_valid() -> None:
    # Not recorded sex, no women's health data: valid.
    patient = _patient(sex=None)
    assert patient.antecedents.pregnancy_contraception == ""
    assert patient.antecedents.breastfeeding == ""


# ----- display_name ------------------------------------------------


def test_display_name_falls_back_to_full_name() -> None:
    patient = _patient(full_name="Gabriel Silva", social_name=None)
    assert patient.display_name() == "Gabriel Silva"


def test_display_name_prefers_social_name() -> None:
    patient = _patient(full_name="Gabriel Silva", social_name="Gabi")
    assert patient.display_name() == "Gabi"


# ----- age ----------------------------------------------------------


def test_age_is_none_when_birth_date_is_none() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=None)
    assert patient.age() is None


def test_age_with_birthday_already_occurred() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=date(1990, 5, 12))
    # Reference: 2026-06-01. Birthday already occurred → 36.
    assert patient.age(at_date=date(2026, 6, 1)) == 36


def test_age_with_birthday_not_yet_occurred() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=date(1990, 12, 31))
    # Reference: 2026-06-01. Birthday not yet occurred → 35.
    assert patient.age(at_date=date(2026, 6, 1)) == 35


def test_age_on_birthday() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=date(1990, 5, 12))
    # Reference: 2026-05-12, exactly the birthday → 36.
    assert patient.age(at_date=date(2026, 5, 12)) == 36


def test_age_on_day_before_birthday() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=date(1990, 5, 12))
    # Reference: 2026-05-11, one day before → 35.
    assert patient.age(at_date=date(2026, 5, 11)) == 35


# ----- summary ------------------------------------------------------


def test_summary_returns_patient_summary() -> None:
    patient = _patient(
        patient_id="pat-007",
        full_name="Gabriel Silva",
        birth_date=date(1990, 5, 12),
        sex=PatientSex.MALE,
    )
    summary = patient.summary()
    assert isinstance(summary, PatientSummary)
    assert summary.patient_id == PatientId("pat-007")
    assert summary.display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert summary.birth_date == date(1990, 5, 12)
    assert summary.sex is PatientSex.MALE


def test_summary_uses_display_name() -> None:
    patient = _patient(
        full_name="Gabriel Silva",
        social_name="Gabi",
    )
    summary = patient.summary()
    assert summary.display_name == "Gabi"


def test_summary_with_all_optional_fields_none() -> None:
    patient = _patient(birth_date=None, sex=None)
    summary = patient.summary()
    assert summary.birth_date is None
    assert summary.sex is None


# ----- Representation -----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_mentions_id_and_display_name() -> None:
    patient = _patient("pat-007", full_name="Gabriel Silva")
    text = repr(patient)
    assert "pat-007" in text
    assert "Gabriel Silva" in text


def test_repr_uses_social_name_when_present() -> None:
    patient = _patient(
        "pat-007",
        full_name="Gabriel Silva",
        social_name="Gabi",
    )
    assert "Gabi" in repr(patient)



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/domain/patient/test_patient_summary.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for PatientSummary.

PatientSummary is a lightweight read-only projection of a Patient.
It carries only the fields needed to identify and display the
patient in contexts where the full record is not required, such as
project listings, tooltips, breadcrumbs, and the ProjectContext
handed to the Workspace.

The summary is a value object. It is derived from a Patient, but it
is not the Patient itself, and it does not carry any clinical data
(no medical history, no imaging references).

See ADR-0004 §13 for the design rationale.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import date

import pytest

from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _summary(**overrides) -> PatientSummary:
    """Build a PatientSummary with sensible defaults.

    Callers may override any field by keyword.
    """
    base: dict = {
        "patient_id": PatientId("pat-001"),
        "display_name": "Gabriel Silva",
        "birth_date": date(1990, 5, 12),
        "sex": PatientSex.MALE,
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return PatientSummary(**base)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_summary_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    s = _summary()
    assert s.patient_id == PatientId("pat-001")
    assert s.display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert s.birth_date == date(1990, 5, 12)
    assert s.sex is PatientSex.MALE


def test_summary_is_frozen() -> None:
    s = _summary()
    attr = "display_name"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(s, attr, "Other")


def test_summary_is_hashable() -> None:
    a = _summary()
    b = _summary()
    # Equal summaries have equal hashes, so they can be used in sets
    # and as dict keys.
    assert hash(a) == hash(b)


def test_two_summaries_with_same_data_are_equal() -> None:
    a = _summary()
    b = _summary()
    assert a == b


# ----- Validation ---------------------------------------------------


def test_empty_display_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="display_name"):
        _summary(display_name="")


def test_whitespace_only_display_name_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="whitespace"):
        _summary(display_name="   ")


# ----- Optional fields ----------------------------------------------


def test_birth_date_can_be_none() -> None:
    s = _summary(birth_date=None)
    assert s.birth_date is None


def test_sex_can_be_none() -> None:
    s = _summary(sex=None)
    assert s.sex is None


def test_summary_with_all_optional_fields_none() -> None:
    s = _summary(birth_date=None, sex=None)
    assert s.birth_date is None
    assert s.sex is None
    # Required fields remain present.
    assert s.patient_id == PatientId("pat-001")
    assert s.display_name == "Gabriel Silva"


# ----- Equality and hashing ----------------------------------------


def test_summaries_with_different_display_names_are_not_equal() -> None:
    a = _summary(display_name="A")
    b = _summary(display_name="B")
    assert a != b


def test_summaries_with_different_ids_are_not_equal() -> None:
    a = _summary(patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"))
    b = _summary(patient_id=PatientId("pat-002"))
    assert a != b


# ----- Representation ----------------------------------------------


def test_repr_mentions_key_fields() -> None:
    s = _summary()
    text = repr(s)
    assert "pat-001" in text
    assert "Gabriel Silva" in text


# ----- Boundaries ---------------------------------------------------


def test_summary_does_not_carry_clinical_data() -> None:
    """The summary intentionally excludes clinical fields.

    The projection exists to answer "who is this patient?", not
    "what is this patient's clinical state?". Fields such as
    medical history, medications, allergies, and section value
    objects must never become part of the summary. This test
    documents and guards that boundary.
    """
    forbidden = {
        "medical_history",
        "medications",
        "allergies",
        "comorbidities",
        "antecedents",
        "identity",
        "documents",
        "filiation",
        "address",
        "complaint",
        "habits",
    }
    fields = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(PatientSummary)}
    assert fields.isdisjoint(forbidden)


def test_summary_only_carries_the_expected_fields() -> None:
    """The summary has exactly four fields, by design.

    Adding a field to PatientSummary is a deliberate decision: it
    increases the weight of every listing, tooltip, and breadcrumb
    that carries the summary. This test guards against accidental
    additions.
    """
    fields = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(PatientSummary)}
    assert fields == {
        "patient_id",
        "display_name",
        "birth_date",
        "sex",
    }
